Register blocking lets a\n// single staged tile of j-bodies serve several i-bodies, which both cuts\n// shared-memory traffic and exposes independent FP64 chains for latency hiding.\nconstexpr int UNROLL_I = 4;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(expr)                                                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                                                               \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (expr);                                                                               \\\n        if                                                                                        \\\n    } while (false)\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays mirror of the body list, as used on the device.\nstruct DeviceBodies {\n    // Positions are double buffered so that a single kernel can compute forces\n    // from the old positions and write the integrated positions without needing\n    // a global barrier between the force and integration phases.\n    double* pos[2][3] = {{nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}, {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}};\n    double* vel[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n    int cur = 0;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// One full simulation step: force accumulation followed by integration.\n//\n// Thread t of block b owns the bodies b*(BLOCK*K)+t + m*BLOCK for m in [0,K).\n// Each block walks the j-bodies in ascending order, one BLOCK-sized tile at a\n// time, so every force sum is accumulated in exactly the same order as the\n// sequential reference implementation.\ntemplate <int K>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK) nbodyStepKernel(const double* __restrict__ pxIn,\n    const double* __restrict__ pyIn, const double* __restrict__ pzIn, double* __restrict__ pxOut,\n    double* __restrict__ pyOut, double* __restrict__ pzOut, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int base = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * (BLOCK * K) + tid;\n\n    double px[K], py[K], pz[K];\n    double fx[K], fy[K], fz[K];\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n        const int i = base + m * BLOCK;\n        // Out-of-range lanes participate in the tile loads and barriers but\n        // their results are discarded, so any position value will do.\n        const int src = (i < n) ? i : 0;\n        px[m] = pxIn[src];\n        py[m] = pyIn[src];\n        pz[m] = pzIn[src];\n        fx[m] = 0.0;\n        fy[m] = 0.0;\n        fz[m] = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    for (int tile = 0; tile < n; tile += BLOCK) {\n        const int tileN = min(BLOCK, n - tile);\n        if (tid < tileN) {\n            sx[tid] = pxIn[tile + tid];\n            sy[tid] = pyIn[tile + tid];\n            sz[tid] = pzIn[tile + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (tileN == BLOCK) {\n            // Fast path: fully known trip count, no bounds test in the body.\n#pragma unroll 8\n            for (int j = 0; j < BLOCK; ++j) {\n                const double jx = sx[j], jy = sy[j], jz = sz[j];\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n                    const double dx = jx - px[m];\n                    const double dy = jy - py[m];\n                    const double dz = jz - pz[m];\n                    const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                    const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n                    fx[m] += dx * invDist3;\n                    fy[m] += dy * invDist3;\n                    fz[m] += dz * invDist3;\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int j = 0; j < tileN; ++j) {\n                const double jx = sx[j], jy = sy[j], jz = sz[j];\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n                    const double dx = jx - px[m];\n                    const double dy = jy - py[m];\n                    const double dz = jz - pz[m];\n                    const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                    const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n                    fx[m] += dx * invDist3;\n                    fy[m] += dy * invDist3;\n                    fz[m] += dz * invDist3;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n        const int i = base + m * BLOCK;\n        if (i < n) {\n            const double nvx = vx[i] + DT * fx[m];\n            const double nvy = vy[i] + DT * fy[m];\n            const double nvz = vz[i] + DT * fz[m];\n            vx[i] = nvx;\n            vy[i] = nvy;\n            vz[i] = nvz;\n            pxOut[i] = px[m] + nvx * DT;\n            pyOut[i] = py[m] + nvy * DT;\n            pzOut[i] = pz[m] + nvz * DT;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Per-body contribution to the total energy: kinetic energy of body i plus the\n// potential energy of all pairs (i, j) with j > i, matching the reference\n// implementation's pair enumeration.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK) energyKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n    const double* __restrict__ pz, const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n    const double* __restrict__ vz, double* __restrict__ kinetic, double* __restrict__ potential, const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int i = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK + tid;\n    const int src = (i < n) ? i : 0;\n\n    const double ix = px[src], iy = py[src], iz = pz[src];\n    double pot = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tile = 0; tile < n; tile += BLOCK) {\n        const int tileN = min(BLOCK, n - tile);\n        if (tid < tileN) {\n            sx[tid] = px[tile + tid];\n            sy[tid] = py[tile + tid];\n            sz[tid] = pz[tile + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < tileN; ++j) {\n            if (tile + j > i) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - ix;\n i < n; ++i) {\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n\n        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;\n        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;\n        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;\n        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;\n        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    for (int i = 1; Register blocking lets one\n// staged tile of j-bodies serve several i-bodies, cutting shared-memory traffic\n// and exposing independent FP64 chains for latency hiding.\nconstexpr int UNROLL_I = 4;\n// Below this body count the per-step cross-device position exchange and the\n// extra kernel launches cost more than the added compute throughput, so the\n// simulation stays on a single GPU.\nconstexpr int MULTI_GPU_MIN_BODIES = 8192;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(expr)                                                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                                                               \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (expr);                                                                               \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                                     \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err_));                \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                                                        \\\n        }                                                                                                              \\\n    } while (false)\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// One full simulation step for the bodies in [iStart, iEnd): force accumulation\n// followed by integration.\n//\n// Thread t of block b owns the bodies iStart + b*(BLOCK*K) + t + m*BLOCK for m\n// in [0, K). Every block walks the j-bodies in ascending order, one BLOCK-sized\n// tile at a time, so each force sum is accumulated in exactly the same order as\n// the sequential reference implementation - the partitioning across threads,\n// blocks and GPUs does not reassociate any sum.\ntemplate <int K>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK) nbodyStepKernel(const double* __restrict__ pxIn,\n    const double* __restrict__ pyIn, const double* __restrict__ pzIn, double* __restrict__ pxOut,\n    double* __restrict__ pyOut, double* __restrict__ pzOut, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n, const int iStart, const int iEnd) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int base = iStart + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * (BLOCK * K) + tid;\n\n    double px[K], py[K], pz[K];\n    double fx[K], fy[K], fz[K];\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n        const int i = base + m * BLOCK;\n        // Out-of-range lanes still take part in the tile loads and barriers but\n        // their results are discarded, so any in-bounds position will do.\n        const int src = (i < iEnd) ? i : 0;\n        px[m] = pxIn[src];\n        py[m] = pyIn[src];\n        pz[m] = pzIn[src];\n        fx[m] = 0.0;\n        fy[m] = 0.0;\n        fz[m] = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    for (int tile = 0; tile < n; tile += BLOCK) {\n        const int tileN = min(BLOCK, n - tile);\n        if (tid < tileN) {\n            sx[tid] = pxIn[tile + tid];\n            sy[tid] = pyIn[tile + tid];\n            sz[tid] = pzIn[tile + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (tileN == BLOCK) {\n            // Fast path: compile-time trip count, no bounds test in the body.\n#pragma unroll 8\n            for (int j = 0; j < BLOCK; ++j) {\n                const double jx = sx[j], jy = sy[j], jz = sz[j];\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n                    const double dx = jx - px[m];\n                    const double dy = jy - py[m];\n                    const double dz = jz - pz[m];\n                    const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                    const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n                    fx[m] += dx * invDist3;\n                    fy[m] += dy * invDist3;\n                    fz[m] += dz * invDist3;\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int j = 0; j < tileN; ++j) {\n                const double jx = sx[j], jy = sy[j], jz = sz[j];\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n                    const double dx = jx - px[m];\n                    const double dy = jy - py[m];\n                    const double dz = jz - pz[m];\n                    const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                    const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n                    fx[m] += dx * invDist3;\n                    fy[m] += dy * invDist3;\n                    fz[m] += dz * invDist3;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n        const int i = base + m * BLOCK;\n        if (i < iEnd) {\n            const double nvx = vx[i] + DT * fx[m];\n            const double nvy = vy[i] + DT * fy[m];\n            const double nvz = vz[i] + DT * fz[m];\n            vx[i] = nvx;\n            vy[i] = nvy;\n            vz[i] = nvz;\n            pxOut[i] = px[m] + nvx * DT;\n            pyOut[i] = py[m] + nvy * DT;\n            pzOut[i] = pz[m] + nvz * DT;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Per-body contribution to the total energy: the kinetic energy of body i plus\n// the potential energy of all pairs (i, j) with j > i, matching the reference\n// implementation's pair enumeration and inner summation order.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK) energyKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n    const double* __restrict__ pz, const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n    const double* __restrict__ vz, double* __restrict__ kinetic, double* __restrict__ potential, const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int blockStart = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK;\n    const int i = blockStart + tid;\n    const int src = (i < n) ? i : 0;\n\n    const double ix = px[src], iy = py[src], iz = pz[src];\n    double pot = 0.0;\n\n    // Tiles below blockStart contain only j <= i for every thread in the block,\n    // so they contribute nothing and can be skipped outright.\n    for (int tile = blockStart; tile < n; tile += BLOCK) {\n        const int tileN = min(BLOCK, n - tile);\n        if (tid < tileN) {\n            sx[tid] = px[tile + tid];\n            sy[tid] = py[tile + tid];\n            sz[tid] = pz[tile + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < tileN; ++j) {\n            if (tile + j > i) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - ix;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - iy;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - iz;\n                pot += 1.0 / sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (i < n) {\n        kinetic[i] = 0.5 * (vx[i] * vx[i] + vy[i] * vy[i] + vz[i] * vz[i]);\n        potential[i] = pot;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Multi-GPU simulation driver\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// State owned by one GPU. Every GPU keeps a full copy of the position arrays\n// (it needs all of them to accumulate forces) but only the velocities of its\n// own body slice are ever read or written.\nstruct GpuState {\n    int device = 0;\n    int iStart = 0;\n    int iEnd = 0;\n    // Positions are double buffered so a single kernel can read the previous\n    // positions and write the integrated ones without a global barrier.\n    double* pos[2][3] = {{nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}, {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}};\n    double* vel[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n    cudaEvent_t exchangeDone = nullptr;\n};\n\nclass Simulation {\n  public:\n    Simulation(const int n, const int numDevices) : n_(n), gpus_(numDevices) {\n        const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(double);\n\n        // Partition the bodies into BLOCK-aligned, near-equal contiguous slices\n        // so that every GPU issues fully coalesced accesses.\n        const int totalBlocks = (n_ + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;\n        int assigned = 0;\n        for (int g = 0; g < numDevices; ++g) {\n            const int myBlocks = totalBlocks / numDevices + (g < totalBlocks % numDevices ? 1 : 0);\n            GpuState& s = gpus_[g];\n            s.device = g;\n            s.iStart = assigned;\n            assigned = min(n_, assigned + myBlocks * BLOCK);\n            s.iEnd = assigned;\n\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(g));\n            for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n                for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&s.pos[b][c], bytes));\n                }\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&s.vel[c], bytes));\n            }\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&s.stream));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&s.exchangeDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n        }\n\n        // Enable direct peer access where the hardware supports it; the\n        // exchange copies fall back to host staging automatically otherwise.\n        for (int g = 0; g < numDevices; ++g) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(g));\n            for (int h = 0; h < numDevices; ++h) {\n                int canAccess = 0;\n                if (g == h) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceCanAccessPeer(&canAccess, g, h));\n                if (canAccess) {\n                    const cudaError_t err = cudaDeviceEnablePeerAccess(h, 0);\n                    if (err != cudaSuccess && err != cudaErrorPeerAccessAlreadyEnabled) {\n                        CUDA_CHECK(err);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Register blocking pays off only while there is still enough work to\n        // give every SM several blocks; otherwise favour more, smaller blocks.\n        int smCount = 1;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&smCount, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, 0));\n        const int perGpu = (n_ + numDevices - 1) / numDevices;\n        unrolled_ = perGpu >= BLOCK * UNROLL_I * smCount * 2;\n\n        scratch_.resize(static_cast<size_t>(n_) * 6);\n    }\n\n    ~Simulation() {\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            cudaSetDevice(s.device);\n            for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n                for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                    cudaFree(s.pos[b][c]);\n                }\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                cudaFree(s.vel[c]);\n            }\n            cudaStreamDestroy(s.stream);\n            cudaEventDestroy(s.exchangeDone);\n        }\n    }\n\n    void upload(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n        for (int i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {\n            scratch_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n            scratch_[n_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n            scratch_[2 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n            scratch_[3 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;\n            scratch_[4 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;\n            scratch_[5 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;\n        }\n        const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(double);\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(\n                    s.pos[0][c], &scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(c) * n_], bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.stream));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(\n                    s.vel[c], &scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(3 + c) * n_], bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.stream));\n            }\n            // Give the event an initial recording so the first step's waits are\n            // well defined.\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(s.exchangeDone, s.stream));\n        }\n        cur_ = 0;\n    }\n\n    void step() {\n        const int in = cur_;\n        const int out = 1 - cur_;\n        const int numDevices = static_cast<int>(gpus_.size());\n\n        // All cross-device waits are issued before any event is re-recorded, so\n        // every wait refers to the previous step's exchange and the GPUs run\n        // this step's kernels concurrently.\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            if (numDevices == 1) {\n                break;\n            }\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            for (const GpuState& other : gpus_) {\n                if (other.device != s.device) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(s.stream, other.exchangeDone, 0));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            const int count = s.iEnd - s.iStart;\n            if (count > 0) {\n                const int perThread = unrolled_ ? UNROLL_I : 1;\n                const int blocks = (count + BLOCK * perThread - 1) / (BLOCK * perThread);\n                if (unrolled_) {\n                    nbodyStepKernel<UNROLL_I><<<blocks, BLOCK, 0, s.stream>>>(s.pos[in][0], s.pos[in][1], s.pos[in][2],\n                        s.pos[out][0], s.pos[out][1], s.pos[out][2], s.vel[0], s.vel[1], s.vel[2], n_, s.iStart,\n                        s.iEnd);\n                } else {\n                    nbodyStepKernel<1><<<blocks, BLOCK, 0, s.stream>>>(s.pos[in][0], s.pos[in][1], s.pos[in][2],\n                        s.pos[out][0], s.pos[out][1], s.pos[out][2], s.vel[0], s.vel[1], s.vel[2], n_, s.iStart,\n                        s.iEnd);\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Broadcast this GPU's freshly integrated position slice to all the\n            // others so that every GPU has the complete state for the next step.\n            if (numDevices > 1) {\n                const size_t sliceBytes = static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(double);\n                for (GpuState& other : gpus_) {\n                    if (other.device == s.device || count == 0) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyPeerAsync(other.pos[out][c] + s.iStart, other.device,\n                            s.pos[out][c] + s.iStart, s.device, sliceBytes, s.stream));\n                    }\n                }\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(s.exchangeDone, s.stream));\n            }\n        }\n\n        cur_ = out;\n    }\n\n    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            const size_t sliceBytes = static_cast<size_t>(s.iEnd - s.iStart) * sizeof(double);\n            if (sliceBytes == 0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(c) * n_ + s.iStart],\n                    s.pos[cur_][c] + s.iStart, sliceBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.stream));\n      bodies[i].pos.x = scratch_[i];\n            bodies[i].pos.y = scratch_[n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].pos.z = scratch_[2 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.x = scratch_[3 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.y = scratch_[4 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.z = scratch_[5 * n_ + i];\n        }\n    }\n\n  private:\n    int n_;\n    std::vector<GpuState> gpus_;\n    std::vector<double> energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies                                                                               \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                                     \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err_));                \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                                                        \\\n        }                                                                                                              \\\n    } while (false)\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// One full simulation step for the bodies in [iStart, iEnd): force accumulation\n// followed by integration.\n//\n// Thread t of block b owns the bodies iStart + b*(BLOCK*K) + t + m*BLOCK for m\n// in [0, K). Every block walks the j-bodies in ascending order, one BLOCK-sized\n// tile at a time, so each force sum is accumulated in exactly the same order as\n// the sequential reference implementation - the partitioning across threads,\n// blocks and GPUs does not reassociate any sum.\ntemplate <int K>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK) nbodyStepKernel(const double* __restrict__ pxIn,\n    const double* __restrict__ pyIn, const double* __restrict__ pzIn, double* __restrict__ pxOut,\n    double* __restrict__ pyOut, double* __restrict__ pzOut, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n, const int iStart, const int iEnd) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int base = iStart + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * (BLOCK * K) + tid;\n\n    double px[K], py[K], pz[K];\n    double fx[K], fy[K], fz[K];\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n        const int i = base + m * BLOCK;\n        // Out-of-range lanes still take part in the tile loads and barriers but\n        // their results are discarded, so any in-bounds position will do.\n        const int src = (i < iEnd) ? i : 0;\n        px[m] = pxIn[src];\n        py[m] = pyIn[src];\n        pz[m] = pzIn[src];\n        fx[m] = 0.0;\n        fy[m] = 0.0;\n        fz[m] = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    for (int tile = 0; tile < n; tile += BLOCK) {\n        const int tileN = min(BLOCK, n - tile);\n        if (tid < tileN) {\n            sx[tid] = pxIn[tile + tid];\n            sy[tid] = pyIn[tile + tid];\n            sz[tid] = pzIn[tile + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (tileN == BLOCK) {\n            // Fast path: compile-time trip count, no bounds test in the body.\n#pragma unroll 8\n            for (int j = 0; j < BLOCK; ++j) {\n                const double jx = sx[j], jy = sy[j], jz = sz[j];\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n                    const double dx = jx - px[m];\n                    const double dy = jy - py[m];\n                    const double dz = jz - pz[m];\n                    const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                    const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n                    fx[m] += dx * invDist3;\n                    fy[m] += dy * invDist3;\n                    fz[m] += dz * invDist3;\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int j = 0; j < tileN; ++j) {\n                const double jx = sx[j], jy = sy[j], jz = sz[j];\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n                    const double dx = jx - px[m];\n                    const double dy = jy - py[m];\n                    const double dz = jz - pz[m];\n                    const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                    const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                    const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n                    fx[m] += dx * invDist3;\n                    fy[m] += dy * invDist3;\n                    fz[m] += dz * invDist3;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < K; ++m) {\n        const int i = base + m * BLOCK;\n        if (i < iEnd) {\n            const double nvx = vx[i] + DT * fx[m];\n            const double nvy = vy[i] + DT * fy[m];\n            const double nvz = vz[i] + DT * fz[m];\n            vx[i] = nvx;\n            vy[i] = nvy;\n            vz[i] = nvz;\n            pxOut[i] = px[m] + nvx * DT;\n            pyOut[i] = py[m] + nvy * DT;\n            pzOut[i] = pz[m] + nvz * DT;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Per-body contribution to the total energy: the kinetic energy of body i plus\n// the potential energy of all pairs (i, j) with j > i, matching the reference\n// implementation's pair enumeration and inner summation order.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK) energyKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n    const double* __restrict__ pz, const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n    const double* __restrict__ vz, double* __restrict__ kinetic, double* __restrict__ potential, const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int blockStart = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK;\n    const int i = blockStart + tid;\n    const int src = (i < n) ? i : 0;\n\n    const double ix = px[src], iy = py[src], iz = pz[src];\n    double pot = 0.0;\n\n    // Tiles below blockStart contain only j <= i for every thread in the block,\n    // so they contribute nothing and can be skipped outright.\n    for (int tile = blockStart; tile < n; tile += BLOCK) {\n        const int tileN = min(BLOCK, n - tile);\n        if (tid < tileN) {\n            sx[tid] = px[tile + tid];\n            sy[tid] = py[tile + tid];\n            sz[tid] = pz[tile + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < tileN; ++j) {\n            if (tile + j > i) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - ix;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - iy;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - iz;\n                pot += 1.0 / sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (i < n) {\n        kinetic[i] = 0.5 * (vx[i] * vx[i] + vy[i] * vy[i] + vz[i] * vz[i]);\n        potential[i] = pot;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Multi-GPU simulation driver\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// State owned by one GPU. Every GPU keeps a full copy of the position arrays\n// (it needs all of them to accumulate forces) but only the velocities of its\n// own body slice are ever read or written.\nstruct GpuState {\n    int device = 0;\n    int iStart = 0;\n    int iEnd = 0;\n    // Positions are double buffered so a single kernel can read the previous\n    // positions and write the integrated ones without a global barrier.\n    double* pos[2][3] = {{nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}, {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}};\n    double* vel[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n    cudaEvent_t exchangeDone = nullptr;\n};\n\nclass Simulation {\n  public:\n    Simulation(const int n, const int numDevices) : n_(n), gpus_(numDevices) {\n        const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(double);\n\n        // Partition the bodies into BLOCK-aligned, near-equal contiguous slices\n        // so that every GPU issues fully coalesced accesses.\n        const int totalBlocks = (n_ + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;\n        int assigned = 0;\n        for (int g = 0; g < numDevices; ++g) {\n            const int myBlocks = totalBlocks / numDevices + (g < totalBlocks % numDevices ? 1 : 0);\n            GpuState& s = gpus_[g];\n            s.device = g;\n            s.iStart = assigned;\n            assigned = min(n_, assigned + myBlocks * BLOCK);\n            s.iEnd = assigned;\n\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(g));\n            for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n                for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&s.pos[b][c], bytes));\n                }\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&s.vel[c], bytes));\n            }\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&s.stream));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&s.exchangeDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n        }\n\n        // Enable direct peer access where the hardware supports it; the\n        // exchange copies fall back to host staging automatically otherwise.\n        for (int g = 0; g < numDevices; ++g) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(g));\n            for (int h = 0; h < numDevices; ++h) {\n                int canAccess = 0;\n                if (g == h) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceCanAccessPeer(&canAccess, g, h));\n                if (canAccess) {\n                    const cudaError_t err = cudaDeviceEnablePeerAccess(h, 0);\n                    if (err != cudaSuccess && err != cudaErrorPeerAccessAlreadyEnabled) {\n                        CUDA_CHECK(err);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Register blocking pays off only while there is still enough work to\n        // give every SM several blocks; otherwise favour more, smaller blocks.\n        int smCount = 1;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&smCount, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, 0));\n        const int perGpu = (n_ + numDevices - 1) / numDevices;\n        unrolled_ = perGpu >= BLOCK * UNROLL_I * smCount * 2;\n\n        scratch_.resize(static_cast<size_t>(n_) * 6);\n    }\n\n    ~Simulation() {\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            cudaSetDevice(s.device);\n            for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n                for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                    cudaFree(s.pos[b][c]);\n                }\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                cudaFree(s.vel[c]);\n            }\n            cudaStreamDestroy(s.stream);\n            cudaEventDestroy(s.exchangeDone);\n        }\n    }\n\n    void upload(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n        for (int i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {\n            scratch_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n            scratch_[n_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n            scratch_[2 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n            scratch_[3 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;\n            scratch_[4 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;\n            scratch_[5 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;\n        }\n        const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(double);\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(\n                    s.pos[0][c], &scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(c) * n_], bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.stream));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(\n                    s.vel[c], &scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(3 + c) * n_], bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.stream));\n            }\n            // Give the event an initial recording so the first step's waits are\n            // well defined.\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(s.exchangeDone, s.stream));\n        }\n        cur_ = 0;\n    }\n\n    void step() {\n        const int in = cur_;\n        const int out = 1 - cur_;\n        const int numDevices = static_cast<int>(gpus_.size());\n\n        // All cross-device waits are issued before any event is re-recorded, so\n        // every wait refers to the previous step's exchange and the GPUs run\n        // this step's kernels concurrently.\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            if (numDevices == 1) {\n                break;\n            }\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            for (const GpuState& other : gpus_) {\n                if (other.device != s.device) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(s.stream, other.exchangeDone, 0));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            const int count = s.iEnd - s.iStart;\n            if (count > 0) {\n                const int perThread = unrolled_ ? UNROLL_I : 1;\n                const int blocks = (count + BLOCK * perThread - 1) / (BLOCK * perThread);\n                if (unrolled_) {\n                    nbodyStepKernel<UNROLL_I><<<blocks, BLOCK, 0, s.stream>>>(s.pos[in][0], s.pos[in][1], s.pos[in][2],\n                        s.pos[out][0], s.pos[out][1], s.pos[out][2], s.vel[0], s.vel[1], s.vel[2], n_, s.iStart,\n                        s.iEnd);\n                } else {\n                    nbodyStepKernel<1><<<blocks, BLOCK, 0, s.stream>>>(s.pos[in][0], s.pos[in][1], s.pos[in][2],\n                        s.pos[out][0], s.pos[out][1], s.pos[out][2], s.vel[0], s.vel[1], s.vel[2], n_, s.iStart,\n                        s.iEnd);\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Broadcast this GPU's freshly integrated position slice to all the\n            // others so that every GPU has the complete state for the next step.\n            if (numDevices > 1) {\n                const size_t sliceBytes = static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(double);\n                for (GpuState& other : gpus_) {\n                    if (other.device == s.device || count == 0) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyPeerAsync(other.pos[out][c] + s.iStart, other.device,\n                            s.pos[out][c] + s.iStart, s.device, sliceBytes, s.stream));\n                    }\n                }\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(s.exchangeDone, s.stream));\n            }\n        }\n\n        cur_ = out;\n    }\n\n    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            const size_t sliceBytes = static_cast<size_t>(s.iEnd - s.iStart) * sizeof(double);\n            if (sliceBytes == 0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(c) * n_ + s.iStart],\n                    s.pos[cur_][c] + s.iStart, sliceBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.stream));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(3 + c) * n_ + s.iStart],\n                    s.vel[c] + s.iStart, sliceBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.stream));\n            }\n        }\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.stream));\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {\n            bodies[i].pos.x = scratch_[i];\n            bodies[i].pos.y = scratch_[n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].pos.z = scratch_[2 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.x = scratch_[3 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.y = scratch_[4 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.z = scratch_[5 * n_ + i];\n        }\n    }\n\n  private:\n    int n_;\n    std::vector<GpuState> gpus_;\n    std::vector<double> scratch_;\n    int cur_ = 0;\n    bool unrolled_ = false;\n};\n\n// Total energy of the system, evaluated on a single GPU.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(bodies.size());\n    const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double);\n\n    std::vector<double> host(static_cast<size_t>(n) * 6);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        host[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n        host[n + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n        host[2 * n + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n        host[3 * n + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;\n        host[4 * n + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;\n        host[5 * n + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));\n    double* buf[8] = {};\n    for (double*& p : buf) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&p, bytes));\n    }\n    for (int c = 0; c < 6; ++c) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(buf[c], &host[static_cast<size_t>(c) * n], bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n\n    const int blocks = (n + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;\n    energyKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK>>>(buf[0], buf[1], buf[2], buf[3], buf[4], buf[5], buf[6], buf[7], n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    std::vector<double> kinetic(n), potential(n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kinetic.data(), buf[6], bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(potential.data(), buf[7], bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    for (double* p : buf) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(p));\n    }\n\n    // Accumulate in the reference implementation's order: every kinetic term\n    // first, then the pair potentials in ascending body order.\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += kinetic[i];\n    }\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy -= potential[i];\n    }\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n    printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Initialize bodies\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    if (numBodies > 0) {\n        int deviceCount = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n        if (deviceCount < 1) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n        int numDevices = numBodies >= MULTI_GPU_MIN_BODIES ? deviceCount : 1;\n        // Keep at least one BLOCK-sized slice of work per GPU.\n        numDevices = min(numDevices, (numBodies + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK);\n        printf(\"CUDA devices used: %d\\n\", numDevices);\n\n        // Device setup happens before timing, mirroring the reference\n        // implementation which excludes allocation from the measured region.\n        Simulation sim(numBodies, numDevices);\n        for (int g = 0; g < numDevices; ++g) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(g));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n        }\n\n        // Run simulation\n        auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n        sim.upload(bodies);\n        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n            sim.step();\n        }\n        sim.download(bodies);\n\n        auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n        auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n        printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    } else {\n        printf(\"Simulation time: 0 ms\\n\");\n bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n\n        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = numBodies > 0 ? computeTotalEnergy(bodies) : 0.0;\n            printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":12,"oldLines":14,"newStart":12,"newLines":18,"lines":[" constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;"," constexpr double (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                                     \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__, a\n// time, so every force sum is accumulated in exactly the same order as the\n// sequential reference implementation.\ntemplate <int K>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK) nbodyStepKernel(const double* __restrict__ pxIn,\n    const double* __restrict__ pyIn, const double* __restrict__ pzIn, double* __restrict__ pxOut,\n    double* __restrict__ pyOut, double* __restrict__ pzOut, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK];\n    __shared__ Each stream already carries the dependency on the previous\n        // step's exchange, so all GPUs are launched back to back here.\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            const int count = s.iEnd - s.iStart;\n            if (count == 0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            launchStep(s, in, out, count);\n        }\n\n        if (numDevices == 1) {\n            cur_ = out;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Exchange: every GPU pushes its own slice into the staging buffer ...\n        const int buf = stageIdx_;\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            const size_t sliceBytes = static_cast<size_t>(s.iEnd - s.iStart) * sizeof(double);\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(stageBuf_[buf][c] + s.iStart, s.pos[out][c] + s.iStart, sliceBytes,\n                    cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.stream));\n            }\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(s.stageDone, s.stream));\n        }\n\n        // ... and, once all slices have arrived, pulls the complete array back.\n        // A GPU's own slice is rewritten with the value it just staged, so the\n        // round trip is bit-preserving.\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            for (const GpuState& other : gpus_) {\n                if (other.device != s.device) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(s.stream, other.stageDone, 0));\n                }\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(\n                    cudaMemcpyAsync(s.pos[out][c], stageBuf_[buf][c], fullBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.stream));\n            }\n        }\n\n        stageIdx_ = 1 - buf;\n        cur_ = out;\n    }\n\n    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            const size_t sliceBytes = static_cast<size_t>(s.iEnd - s.iStart) * sizeof(double);\n            if (sliceBytes == 0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(c) * n_ + s.iStart],\n                    s.pos[cur_][c] + s.iStart, sliceBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.stream));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(3 + c) * n_ + s.iStart],\n                    s.vel[c] + s.iStart, sliceBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.stream));\n            }\n        }\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.stream));\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {\n            bodies[i].pos.x = scratch_[i];\n            bodies[i].pos.y = scratch_[n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].pos.z = scratch_[2 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.x = scratch_[3 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.y = scratch_[4 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.z = scratch_[5 * n_ + i];\n        }\n    }\n\n  private:\n    int n_;\n    std::vector<GpuState> gpus_;\n    std::vector<double> scratch_;\n    double* stageBuf_[2][3] = {{nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}, {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}};\n    int cur_ = 0;\n    int stageIdx_ = 0;\n    int blockSize_ = MAX_BLOCK;\n\n    // Dispatch to the instantiation matching the chosen block size.\n    void launchStep(const GpuState& s, const int in, const int out, const int count) {\n        const int blocks = (count + blockSize_ - 1) / blockSize_;\n        switch (blockSize_) {\n        case 32:\n            nbodyStepKernel<32><<<blocks, 32, 0, s.stream>>>(s.pos[in][0], s.pos[in][1], s.pos[in][2], s.pos[out][0],\n                s.pos[out][1], s.pos[out][2], s.vel[0], s.vel[1], s.vel[2], n_, s.iStart, s.iEnd);\n            break;\n        case 64:\n            nbodyStepKernel<64><<<blocks, 64, 0, s.stream>>>(s.pos[in][0], s.pos[in][1], s.pos[in][2], s.pos[out][0],\n                s.pos[out][1], s.pos[out][2], s.vel[0], s.vel[1], s.vel[2], n_, s.iStart, s.iEnd);\n            break;\n        default:\n            nbodyStepKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, s.stream>>>(s.pos[in][0], s.pos[in][1], s.pos[in][2], s.pos[out][0],\n                s.pos[out][1], s.pos[out][2], s.vel[0], s.vel[1], s.vel[2], n_, s.iStart, s.iEnd);\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// Total energy of the system, evaluated on a single GPU.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(bodies.size());\n    const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double);","numLines":250,"startLine":186,"totalLines":619}}}

               \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                                                        \\\n        }                                                                                                              \\\n    } while (false)\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// One full simulation step for the bodies in [iStart, iEnd): force\n// accumulation followed by integration.\n//\n// Thread t of block b owns body iStart + b*B + t. Every block walks the\n// j-bodies in ascending order, one B-sized tile at a time, so each force sum is\n// accumulated in exactly the same order as the sequential reference\n// implementation - the split across threads, blocks and GPUs reassociates\n// nothing.\ntemplate <int B>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(B) nbodyStepKernel(const double* __restrict__ pxIn, const double* __restrict__ pyIn,\n    const double* __restrict__ pzIn, double* __restrict__ pxOut, double* __restrict__ pyOut,\n    double* __restrict__ pzOut, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy, double* __restrict__ vz,\n    const int n, const int iStart, const int iEnd) {\n    __shared__ double sx[B];\n    __shared__ double sy[B];\n    __shared__ double sz[B];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int i = iStart + static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * B + tid;\n    // Out-of-range lanes still take part in the tile loads and barriers, but\n    // their results are discarded, so any in-bounds position will do.\n    const int src = (i < iEnd) ? i : 0;\n\n    const double pxi = pxIn[src], pyi = pyIn[src], pzi = pzIn[src];\n    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tile = 0; tile < n; tile += B) {\n        const int tileN = min(B, n - tile);\n        if (tid < tileN) {\n            sx[tid] = pxIn[tile + tid];\n            sy[tid] = pyIn[tile + tid];\n            sz[tid] = pzIn[tile + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // The full-tile path has a compile-time trip count and no bounds test.\n        const int inner = (tileN == B) ? B : tileN;\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (int j = 0; j < inner; ++j) {\n            const double dx = sx[j] - pxi;\n            const double dy = sy[j] - pyi;\n            const double dz = sz[j] - pzi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n            fx += dx * invDist3;\n            fy += dy * invDist3;\n            fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (i < iEnd) {\n        const double nvx = vx[i] + DT * fx;\n        const double nvy = vy[i] + DT * fy;\n        const double nvz = vz[i] + DT * fz;\n        vx[i] = nvx;\n        vy[i] = nvy;\n        vz[i] = nvz;\n        pxOut[i] = pxi + nvx * DT;\n        pyOut[i] = pyi + nvy * DT;\n        pzOut[i] = pzi + nvz * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Per-body contribution to the total energy: the kinetic energy of body i plus\n// the potential energy of all pairs (i, j) with j > i, matching the reference\n// implementation's pair enumeration and inner summation order.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(ENERGY_BLOCK) energyKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n    const double* __restrict__ pz, const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n    const double* __restrict__ vz, double* __restrict__ kinetic, double* __restrict__ potential, const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[ENERGY_BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sy[ENERGY_BLOCK];\n    __shared__ double sz[ENERGY_BLOCK];\n\n    const int tid = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int blockStart = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * ENERGY_BLOCK;\n    const int i = blockStart + tid;\n    const int src = (i < n) ? i : 0;\n\n    const double ix = px[src], iy = py[src], iz = pz[src];\n    double pot = 0.0;\n\n    // Tiles below blockStart contain only j <= i for every thread in the block,\n    // so they contribute nothing and can be skipped outright.\n    for (int tile = blockStart; tile < n; tile += ENERGY_BLOCK) {\n        const int tileN = min(ENERGY_BLOCK, n - tile);\n        if (tid < tileN) {\n            sx[tid] = px[tile + tid];\n            sy[tid] = py[tile + tid];\n            sz[tid] = pz[tile + tid];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int j = 0; j < tileN; ++j) {\n            if (tile + j > i) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - ix;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - iy;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - iz;\n                pot += 1.0 / sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (i < n) {\n        kinetic[i] = 0.5 * (vx[i] * vx[i] + vy[i] * vy[i] + vz[i] * vz[i]);\n        potential[i] = pot;\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Multi-GPU simulation driver\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// State owned by one GPU. Every GPU keeps a full copy of the position arrays\n// (it needs all of them to accumulate forces) but only the velocities of its\n// own body slice are ever read or written.\nstruct GpuState {\n    int device = 0;\n    int iStart = 0;\n    int iEnd = 0;\n    // Positions are double buffered so a single kernel can read the previous\n    // positions and write the integrated ones without a global barrier.\n    double* pos[2][3] = {{nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}, {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}};\n    double* vel[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n    // Signals that this GPU's freshly integrated position slice has landed in\n    // the host staging buffer.\n    cudaEvent_t stageDone = nullptr;\n};\n\nclass Simulation {\n  public:\n    Simulation(const int n, const int numDevices) : n_(n), gpus_(numDevices) {\n        const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(double);\n\n        // Partition the bodies into aligned, near-equal contiguous slices so\n        // that every GPU issues fully coalesced accesses.\n        const int totalChunks = (n_ + SLICE_ALIGN - 1) / SLICE_ALIGN;\n        int assigned = 0;\n        for (int g = 0; g < numDevices; ++g) {\n            const int myChunks = totalChunks / numDevices + (g < totalChunks % numDevices ? 1 : 0);\n            GpuState& s = gpus_[g];\n            s.device = g;\n            s.iStart = assigned;\n            assigned = std::min(n_, assigned + myChunks * SLICE_ALIGN);\n            s.iEnd = assigned;\n\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(g));\n            for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n                for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&s.pos[b][c], bytes));\n                }\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&s.vel[c], bytes));\n            }\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&s.stream));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&s.stageDone, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n        }\n\n        // Pinned, portable staging buffers for the per-step position exchange.\n        // These GeForce-class GPUs sit on separate PCIe root complexes, where\n        // cudaMemcpyPeer falls back to a *synchronous* staged copy that stalls\n        // the issuing thread; routing the exchange through pinned host memory\n        // with ordinary async copies keeps every GPU's stream running ahead.\n        // Two buffers are used in alternation so that a GPU staging step s+1\n        // cannot overwrite data another GPU is still reading for step s.\n        if (numDevices > 1) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));\n            for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n                for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&stageBuf_[b][c], bytes, cudaHostAllocPortable));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Pick the block size that leaves the least of the final wave idle for\n        // the largest slice any GPU has to process.\n        int smCount = 1;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&smCount, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, 0));\n        int maxRows = 0;\n        for (const GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            maxRows = std::max(maxRows, s.iEnd - s.iStart);\n        }\n        double bestCost = -1.0;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < std::size(BLOCK_CANDIDATES); ++c) {\n            const int b = BLOCK_CANDIDATES[c];\n            const int waves = (maxRows + smCount * b - 1) / (smCount * b);\n            const double cost = static_cast<double>(waves) * b * BLOCK_OVERHEAD[c];\n            if (bestCost < 0.0 || cost < bestCost) {\n                bestCost = cost;\n                blockSize_ = b;\n            }\n        }\n\n        scratch_.resize(static_cast<size_t>(n_) * 6);\n    }\n\n    ~Simulation() {\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            cudaSetDevice(s.device);\n            for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n                for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                    cudaFree(s.pos[b][c]);\n                }\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                cudaFree(s.vel[c]);\n            }\n            cudaStreamDestroy(s.stream);\n            cudaEventDestroy(s.stageDone);\n        }\n        for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                if (stageBuf_[b][c] != nullptr) {\n                    cudaFreeHost(stageBuf_[b][c]);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    void upload(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n        for (int i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {\n            scratch_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n            scratch_[n_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n            scratch_[2 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n            scratch_[3 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;\n            scratch_[4 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;\n            scratch_[5 * n_ + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;\n        }\n        const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(double);\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(\n                    s.pos[0][c], &scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(c) * n_], bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.stream));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(\n                    s.vel[c], &scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(3 + c) * n_], bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.stream));\n            }\n        }\n        cur_ = 0;\n        stageIdx_ = 0;\n    }\n\n    void step() {\n        const int in = cur_;\n        const int out = 1 - cur_;\n        const int numDevices = static_cast<int>(gpus_.size());\n        const size_t fullBytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(double);\n\n        // Every GPU integrates its own slice from the complete previous\n        // positions. Each stream already carries the dependency on the previous\n        // step's exchange, so all GPUs are launched back to back here.\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            const int count = s.iEnd - s.iStart;\n            if (count == 0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            launchStep(s, in, out, count);\n        }\n\n        if (numDevices == 1) {\n            cur_ = out;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Exchange: every GPU pushes its own slice into the staging buffer ...\n        const int buf = stageIdx_;\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            const size_t sliceBytes = static_cast<size_t>(s.iEnd - s.iStart) * sizeof(double);\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(stageBuf_[buf][c] + s.iStart, s.pos[out][c] + s.iStart, sliceBytes,\n                    cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.stream));\n            }\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(s.stageDone, s.stream));\n        }\n\n        // ... and, once all slices have arrived, pulls the complete array back.\n        // A GPU's own slice is rewritten with the value it just staged, so the\n        // round trip is bit-preserving.\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            for (const GpuState& other : gpus_) {\n                if (other.device != s.device) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(s.stream, other.stageDone, 0));\n                }\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(\n                    cudaMemcpyAsync(s.pos[out][c], stageBuf_[buf][c], fullBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, s.stream));\n            }\n        }\n\n        stageIdx_ = 1 - buf;\n        cur_ = out;\n    }\n\n    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            const size_t sliceBytes = static_cast<size_t>(s.iEnd - s.iStart) * sizeof(double);\n            if (sliceBytes == 0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(c) * n_ + s.iStart],\n                    s.pos[cur_][c] + s.iStart, sliceBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.stream));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(&scratch_[static_cast<size_t>(3 + c) * n_ + s.iStart],\n                    s.vel[c] + s.iStart, sliceBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, s.stream));\n            }\n        }\n        for (GpuState& s : gpus_) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(s.device));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(s.stream));\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < n_; ++i) {\n            bodies[i].pos.x = scratch_[i];\n            bodies[i].pos.y = scratch_[n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].pos.z = scratch_[2 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.x = scratch_[3 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.y = scratch_[4 * n_ + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.z = scratch_[5 * n_ + i];\n        }\n    }\n\n  private:\n    int n_;\n    std::vector<GpuState> gpus_;\n    std::vector<double> scratch_;\n    double* stageBuf_[2][3] = {{nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}, {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr}};\n    int cur_ = 0;\n    int stageIdx_ = 0;\n    int blockSize_ = MAX_BLOCK;\n\n    // Dispatch to the instantiation matching the chosen block size.\n    void launchStep(const GpuState& s, const int in, const int out, const int count) {\n        const int blocks = (count + blockSize_ - 1) / blockSize_;\n        switch (blockSize_) {\n        case 32:\n            nbodyStepKernel<32><<<blocks, 32, 0, s.stream>>>(s.pos[in][0], s.pos[in][1], s.pos[in][2], s.pos[out][0],\n                s.pos[out][1], s.pos[out][2], s.vel[0], s.vel[1], s.vel[2], n_, s.iStart, s.iEnd);\n            break;\n        case 64:\n            nbodyStepKernel<64><<<blocks, 64, 0, s.stream>>>(s.pos[in][0], s.pos[in][1], s.pos[in][2], s.pos[out][0],\n                s.pos[out][1], s.pos[out][2], s.vel[0], s.vel[1], s.vel[2], n_, s.iStart, s.iEnd);\n            break;\n        default:\n            nbodyStepKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, s.stream>>>(s.pos[in][0], s.pos[in][1], s.pos[in][2], s.pos[out][0],\n                s.pos[out][1], s.pos[out][2], s.vel[0], s.vel[1], s.vel[2], n_, s.iStart, s.iEnd);\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// Total energy of the system, evaluated on a single GPU.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const int n = static_cast<int>(bodies.size());\n    const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n) * sizeof(double);\n\n    std::vector<double> host(static_cast<size_t>(n) * 6);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        host[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n        host[n + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n        host[2 * n + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n        host[3 * n + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;\n        host[4 * n + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;\n        host[5 * n + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));\n    double* buf[8] = {};\n    for (double*& p : buf) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&p, bytes));\n    }\n    for (int c = 0; c < 6; ++c) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(buf[c], &host[static_cast<size_t>(c) * n], bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n\n    const int blocks = (n + ENERGY_BLOCK - 1) / ENERGY_BLOCK;\n    energyKernel<<<blocks, ENERGY_BLOCK>>>(buf[0], buf[1], buf[2], buf[3], buf[4], buf[5], buf[6], buf[7], n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    std::vector<double> kinetic(n), potential(n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(kinetic.data(), buf[6], bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(potential.data(), buf[7], bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    for (double* p : buf) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(p));\n    }\n\n    // Accumulate in the reference implementation's order: every kinetic term\n    // first, then the pair potentials in ascending body order.\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += kinetic[i];\n    }\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy -= potential[i];\n    }\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"
